# Codex — batch I.2 remediation (two math-decision items)

The verifier wave on batch I.2 surfaced two items that need your math authority. Trevor explicitly wants you (not Claude) deciding the math.

## Item 1 — Stage 021 F1 rework

**Status:** the Q6=a apply you did in the prior session ADDED a composed assertion, but the verifier flagged it as tautological. Concretely:

In `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage021_reduced_one_port_normal_form_sympy_audit.py` (and mirrored in mathematica), you added something like:
```python
expect_zero(
    "delta D_2^(odd) composed",
    Dcorr.subs(Gamma_port, a**5/(27*c_s**5)) - (-I * N0 * a**5/(27*c_s**5) * omega**5)
)
```

The problem: `Dcorr = -I * Gamma_port * omega^5 * N0`, so after `Gamma_port → a^5/(27 c_s^5)`, the LHS is exactly `-I * N0 * a^5/(27 c_s^5) * omega^5`. The assertion is `X - X = 0`, which holds trivially without exercising N0's closed form OR Γ_5^port's closed form.

**What the paper claims (eq:app-stage021-wall-odd):**
> δ D_2^{odd}(ω) = -i N_2(0) a^5/(27 c_s^5) ω^5 + O(ω^7)

where N_2(0) is the transfer factor at zero frequency, computed in Section III as the closed form for the reduced one-port:
```
N(0) = (Ω_A² g_W + R g_A)² / (Ω_A² Ω_W² - R²)²    (from sympy line ~210 region)
```

**What you need to do:**

Replace the tautological assertion with one whose RHS uses the **explicit closed form** for both N(0) and Γ_5^port — not the symbol `N0`. The check should look like:

```python
expect_zero(
    "delta D_2^(odd) composed from Section III N(0) closed form and Section IV Gamma5 = a^5/(27 c_s^5)",
    Dcorr.subs(Gamma_port, a**5/(27*c_s**5))
    - (-I * ((OA**2 * gW + R * gA)**2 / (OA**2 * OW**2 - R**2)**2) * a**5/(27*c_s**5) * omega**5)
)
```

This non-tautologically requires N0 (Section III) to actually equal the rational expression, AND Γ_5^port (Section IV) to equal `a^5/(27 c_s^5)`. A future regression in either piece would break this.

Mirror in the Mathematica script using the local symbol names (`radius`, `cS`, `oA`, `oW`, `r`, `gA`, `gW`).

**Authorization:** edit `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage021_*.py` and `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage021_*.wl`. Run both scripts to confirm exit 0. Iterate until clean.

After applying, append `## Applied: F1 (iter2)` to `redteam/directives/stage_021.md` (or note the rework in the existing `## Applied: F1` block if you'd rather just supersede the prior text).

## Item 2 — Stage 023 F2 sign decision

**Status:** the F2 directive prescribed adding a Schur-complement derivation block in both engines for the reduced mass-spring matrix. You correctly blocked F2 in iter1 because the directive's prescription has a sign conflict:

> The exact requested Schur block uses off-diagonal `+Rmix`/`+rMix`, whose adjugate gives a `-2*g_U*g_W*Rmix` cross term, but the existing `Q_expr`/`qExpr` rational numerator uses `+2*g_U*g_W*Rmix`; applying the block as written would introduce a failing assertion.

**What you need to do:**

Read the actual math source so the sign is determined by physics, not by the directive's possibly-typo'd prescription. Specifically:

1. Read `paper/stages/stage_023.tex` — what does the paper's §2 (or whichever section) say about the sign of the off-diagonal `R_mix` term in the U-W coupling Lagrangian? Cite the equation label.
2. Read `notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.md` if a derivation note exists — that's likely where the Schur-complement derivation was first written down with the convention.
3. Read the existing `Q_expr` / `qExpr` in `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.py` and `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.wl` to confirm which sign convention the existing code uses.
4. Decide which sign is correct based on the paper/notes (the source of truth for the math), then either:
   - **(option α)** Apply the Schur block with the corrected sign so the new derivation matches existing `Q_expr` — if the paper supports `+Rmix` and the existing `Q_expr` is right.
   - **(option β)** Fix the existing `Q_expr` cross term sign AND apply the Schur block as originally prescribed — if the paper supports `-Rmix` and the existing `Q_expr` is wrong.

Whichever direction, you're the math authority — derive the sign from the Lagrangian and apply consistently.

**Authorization for Item 2:**
- READ: `paper/stages/stage_023.tex`, `notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.md`, `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.py`, `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.wl`. Also read prior stages 021/022 if relevant for the coupling convention.
- EDIT: `scripts/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.py`, `mathematica/moving_throat_pde_stage023_*.wl`. Plus append a `## Applied: F2` block under `redteam/directives/stage_023.md` documenting which option you chose and why.
- DO NOT edit: paper/tex (if the paper's sign needs changing, mark F2 still blocked with a one-line note for Trevor).
- Run both scripts to confirm exit 0 after editing. Iterate until clean.

   108	
   109	we keep:
   110	
   111	- a wall/worldtube amplitude `q_A`,
   112	- stable BdG support modes `X_(A,alpha)` with frequencies `varpi_(A,alpha)`,
   113	- one or more localized brane-like gauge coordinates `U_(A,r)` with frequencies `Omega_(U,A,r)`,
   114	- one or more mixed `A_w/F_{mu w}/J^w` coordinates `W_(A,r)` with frequencies `Omega_(W,A,r)`,
   115	- internal gauge-sector mixing `R_(A,r)`.
   116	
   117	The reduced quadratic Lagrangian in lane `A` is therefore
   118	
   119	`L_A`
   120	`= (1/2) M_A qdot_A^2 - (1/2) K_A q_A^2`
   121	`  + sum_alpha [ (1/2) Xdot_(A,alpha)^2 - (1/2) varpi_(A,alpha)^2 X_(A,alpha)^2 + c_(A,alpha) q_A X_(A,alpha) ]`
   122	`  + sum_r [ (1/2) Udot_(A,r)^2 - (1/2) Omega_(U,A,r)^2 U_(A,r)^2`
   123	`          + (1/2) Wdot_(A,r)^2 - (1/2) Omega_(W,A,r)^2 W_(A,r)^2`
   124	`          + R_(A,r) U_(A,r) W_(A,r)`
   125	`          + g_(U,A,r) q_A U_(A,r) + g_(W,A,r) q_A W_(A,r) ]`.
   126	
   127	This is the full reduced grouped-lane bundle we need for the next theorem gate.
   128	
   129	It combines, in one place,
   130	
   131	- the geometry wall backbone,
   132	- the BdG support self-energy,
   133	- the conservative localized-Maxwell/mixed self-energy,
   134	- and the outgoing transfer channel.

   137	
   138	## 3. Exact full-bundle low-frequency coefficients
   139	
   140	### 3.1 BdG support contributions
   141	
   142	For each lane `A`, define the exact BdG moments
   143	
   144	`B_(A,0) = sum_alpha c_(A,alpha)^2 / varpi_(A,alpha)^2`,
   145	
   146	`B_(A,2) = sum_alpha c_(A,alpha)^2 / varpi_(A,alpha)^4`,
   147	
   148	`B_(A,4) = sum_alpha c_(A,alpha)^2 / varpi_(A,alpha)^6`.
   149	
   150	These are just the Stage-3 conservative support moments collected lane by lane.
   151	
   152	### 3.2 Conservative Maxwell/mixed contributions
   153	
   154	For each port-active internal pair `(U_(A,r),W_(A,r))`, define
   155	
   156	`Delta_(A,r) = Omega_(U,A,r)^2 Omega_(W,A,r)^2 - R_(A,r)^2`,
   157	
   158	`S_(A,r) = Omega_(U,A,r)^2 + Omega_(W,A,r)^2`,
   159	
   160	`Q_(A,r) = g_(U,A,r)^2 Omega_(W,A,r)^2 + 2 g_(U,A,r) g_(W,A,r) R_(A,r) + g_(W,A,r)^2 Omega_(U,A,r)^2`,
   161	
   162	`G_(A,r) = g_(U,A,r)^2 + g_(W,A,r)^2`.
   163	
   164	Then the conservative low-frequency coefficients contributed by that port are
   165	
   166	`Z_(A,0)^(r) = Q_(A,r) / Delta_(A,r)`,
   167	
   168	`Z_(A,2)^(r) = [ Q_(A,r) S_(A,r) - G_(A,r) Delta_(A,r) ] / Delta_(A,r)^2`,
   169	
   170	`Z_(A,4)^(r) = [ Q_(A,r)(S_(A,r)^2 - Delta_(A,r)) - S_(A,r) G_(A,r) Delta_(A,r) ] / Delta_(A,r)^3`.
   171	
   172	Summing over all internal Maxwell/mixed branches gives
   173	
   174	`Z_(A,n) = sum_r Z_(A,n)^(r)`
   175	
   176	for `n=0,2,4`.
   177	
   178	### 3.3 Outgoing-transfer coefficients
   179	
   180	For each port-active internal pair, the exact outgoing transfer factor begins with
   181	
   182	`N_(A,0)^(r) = [ Omega_(U,A,r)^2 g_(W,A,r) + R_(A,r) g_(U,A,r) ]^2 / Delta_(A,r)^2`.

   115	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116	# II. Full coupled bundle coefficient assembly
   117	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118	
   119	def bundle_coefficient_assembly() -> dict[str, sp.Expr]:
   120	    banner("SECTION II — FULL COUPLED BUNDLE COEFFICIENT ASSEMBLY")
   121	
   122	    subbanner("II.0 — Representative one-port derivation of Z_n and N_n")
   123	    omega = sp.symbols("omega", real=True)
   124	    OmegaU, OmegaW, Rmix, gU, gW = sp.symbols("Omega_U Omega_W R_mix g_U g_W", real=True)
   125	
   126	    Delta_expr = sp.simplify(OmegaU**2 * OmegaW**2 - Rmix**2)
   127	    S_expr = sp.simplify(OmegaU**2 + OmegaW**2)
   128	    Q_expr = sp.simplify(gU**2 * OmegaW**2 + 2 * gU * gW * Rmix + gW**2 * OmegaU**2)
   129	    H_expr = sp.simplify(gU**2 + gW**2)
   130	    P_expr = sp.simplify(OmegaU**2 * gW + Rmix * gU)
   131	
   132	    Z_one_port = sp.expand(
   133	        sp.series(
   134	            (Q_expr - H_expr * omega**2) / (Delta_expr - S_expr * omega**2 + omega**4),
   135	            omega,
   136	            0,
   137	            6,
   138	        ).removeO()
   139	    )
   140	    expect_zero("Z0 one-port formula", Z_one_port.coeff(omega, 0) - Q_expr / Delta_expr)
   141	    expect_zero(
   142	        "Z2 one-port formula",
   143	        Z_one_port.coeff(omega, 2) - (Q_expr * S_expr - H_expr * Delta_expr) / Delta_expr**2,
   144	    )
   145	    expect_zero(
   146	        "Z4 one-port formula",
   147	        Z_one_port.coeff(omega, 4)
   148	        - (Q_expr * (S_expr**2 - Delta_expr) - S_expr * H_expr * Delta_expr) / Delta_expr**3,
   149	    )
   150	
   151	    N_one_port = sp.expand(
   152	        sp.series(
   153	            (P_expr - gW * omega**2) ** 2 / (Delta_expr - S_expr * omega**2 + omega**4) ** 2,
   154	            omega,
   155	            0,
   156	            6,
   157	        ).removeO()
   158	    )
   159	    expect_zero("N0 one-port formula", N_one_port.coeff(omega, 0) - P_expr**2 / Delta_expr**2)
   160	    expect_zero(
   161	        "N2 one-port formula",
   162	        N_one_port.coeff(omega, 2)
   163	        - 2 * P_expr * (P_expr * S_expr - Delta_expr * gW) / Delta_expr**3,
   164	    )
   165	    expect_zero(
   166	        "N4 one-port formula",
   167	        N_one_port.coeff(omega, 4)
   168	        - (
   169	            Delta_expr**2 * gW**2
   170	            - 2 * Delta_expr * P_expr**2
   171	            - 4 * Delta_expr * P_expr * S_expr * gW
   172	            + 3 * P_expr**2 * S_expr**2

    31	**Issue:** The block on lines 313-320 defines `N0_target` as the solution of `mhat^2 * N0/D0 = 54 G c_s^5/(5 a^5 c^5)` for `N0`, then asserts that substituting `N0_target` back into the same equation gives zero. This is algebraically tautological — the assertion cannot fail regardless of whether the universal target is correct. The real verification (Gamma5_port + ratio_target) on lines 322-343 is substantive and is kept.

Keep the `subbanner` header. Keep everything from line 321 onward (the Stage-5 Gamma5_port derivation) unchanged.
    36	
    37	Before:
    38	```python
    39	    subbanner("III.3 — Universal normalization product")

    41	    # Substantive check: N0_target, when substituted into mhat^2 * P0, must

    43	    expect_zero(
    44	        "N0_target reproduces universal normalization",

    52	    # Non-tautological substitution check: the abstract normalization
    53	    # mhat^2 * P0 = 54 G c_s^5/(5 a^5 c^5) must agree with the explicit
    54	    # full-bundle form mhat^2 * N0/(K - B0 - Z0) = 54 G c_s^5/(5 a^5 c^5)
    55	    # after substituting D0 = K - B0 - Z0. This exercises the paper's
    56	    # eq:app-stage023-normalization-test equivalence between abstract P0
    57	    # and explicit (K, B0, Z0) denominators.
    58	    K_sym, B0_sym, Z0_sym = sp.symbols("K_sym B0_sym Z0_sym", positive=True, real=True)
    59	    norm_abstract = mhat**2 * N0 / D0 - 54 * G * c_s**5 / (5 * a**5 * c**5)
    60	    norm_explicit = mhat**2 * N0 / (K_sym - B0_sym - Z0_sym) - 54 * G * c_s**5 / (5 * a**5 * c**5)
    61	    expect_zero(
    62	        "normalization abstract == explicit under D0 = K - B0 - Z0",
    63	        sp.simplify(norm_abstract.subs(D0, K_sym - B0_sym - Z0_sym) - norm_explicit),
    64	    )
    65	```

    72	Clear[kNorm, b0Norm, z0Norm];

   103	
   104	```python
   105	    # Schur-complement derivation of the rational form used below.
   106	    # The (U,W) block of the Lagrangian (paper eq:app-stage023-full-lagrangian
   107	    # restricted to one port) gives the frequency-dependent mass-spring matrix
   108	    #     M(ω) = [[Ω_U^2 - ω^2, R], [R, Ω_W^2 - ω^2]]
   109	    # whose determinant is the rational-function denominator.
   110	    Mblock = sp.Matrix([[OmegaU**2 - omega**2, Rmix], [Rmix, OmegaW**2 - omega**2]])
   111	    det_M = sp.expand(Mblock.det())
   112	    expect_zero(
   113	        "Schur denominator matches Delta - S omega^2 + omega^4",
   114	        sp.expand(det_M - (Delta_expr - S_expr * omega**2 + omega**4)),
   115	    )
   116	    # The Schur reduction of q -> (U,W) -> q gives (g_U, g_W) M(ω)^{-1} (g_U, g_W)^T,
   117	    # which must equal the (Q - H ω^2)/det_M rational form used in Section II.0.

   121	        "Z rational form matches Schur (g_U,g_W) M^{-1} (g_U,g_W)^T",

codex
Stage 023’s paper and derivation note both state the Lagrangian term as `+ R U W` in `eq:app-stage023-full-lagrangian`. That means the frequency-space spring matrix has off-diagonal `-R`, so the existing positive cross term in `Q_expr` is the consistent convention.

 hExpr = gU^2 + gW^2;
 pExpr = omegaU^2*gW + rMix*gU;
 
+(* Schur-complement derivation of the rational form used below.
+   The paper's one-port Lagrangian has +R U W, so the frequency-space
+   spring matrix has off-diagonal -R. Its determinant is the denominator. *)
+mBlock = {{omegaU^2 - omega^2, -rMix}, {-rMix, omegaW^2 - omega^2}};
+detM = Expand[Det[mBlock]];
+expectZero["Schur denominator matches Delta - S omega^2 + omega^4",
+  Expand[detM - (deltaExpr - sExpr*omega^2 + omega^4)]];
+gVec = {gU, gW};
+zSchur = FullSimplify[gVec . Inverse[mBlock] . gVec, Assumptions -> $Assumptions];
+expectZero["Z rational form matches Schur (g_U,g_W) M^{-1} (g_U,g_W)^T",
+  FullSimplify[zSchur - (qExpr - hExpr*omega^2)/detM, Assumptions -> $Assumptions]];
+

+    # Schur-complement derivation of the rational form used below.
+    # The paper's one-port Lagrangian has +R U W, so the frequency-space
+    # spring matrix has off-diagonal -R. Its determinant is the denominator.
+    Mblock = sp.Matrix([[OmegaU**2 - omega**2, -Rmix], [-Rmix, OmegaW**2 - omega**2]])
+    det_M = sp.expand(Mblock.det())
+    expect_zero(
+        "Schur denominator matches Delta - S omega^2 + omega^4",
+        sp.expand(det_M - (Delta_expr - S_expr * omega**2 + omega**4)),
+    )
+    # The Schur reduction of q -> (U,W) -> q gives
+    # (g_U, g_W) M(omega)^(-1) (g_U, g_W)^T.
+    g_vec = sp.Matrix([gU, gW])
+    Z_schur = sp.simplify(((g_vec.T * Mblock.adjugate() * g_vec)[0]) / det_M)
+    expect_zero(
+        "Z rational form matches Schur (g_U,g_W) M^{-1} (g_U,g_W)^T",
+        sp.simplify(Z_schur - (Q_expr - H_expr * omega**2) / det_M),
+    )

+(* Schur-complement derivation of the rational form used below.
+   The paper's one-port Lagrangian has +R U W, so the frequency-space
+   spring matrix has off-diagonal -R. Its determinant is the denominator. *)
+mBlock = {{omegaU^2 - omega^2, -rMix}, {-rMix, omegaW^2 - omega^2}};

+  Expand[detM - (deltaExpr - sExpr*omega^2 + omega^4)]];

+  FullSimplify[zSchur - (qExpr - hExpr*omega^2)/detM, Assumptions -> $Assumptions]];

FINAL STAGE-6 LEDGER
========================================================================================
Verified with SymPy:
  • the grouped real P2 bundle has a natural weighted metric Ggrp = diag(1,2,2)
    with exact orthogonal projectors Pbar, Pa, Pb;
  • any grouped coefficient vector decomposes exactly as
    x = xbar ebar + a_x ea + b_x eb;
  • the full coupled bundle coefficients are
    D_A0 = K_A - B_A0 - Z_A0,
    D_A2 = -(M_A + B_A2 + Z_A2),
    D_A4 = -(B_A4 + Z_A4),
    with an independent outgoing-transfer bundle N_An;
  • the grouped trace/anomaly parts of D_An and N_An inherit linearly from the
    microscopic wall, BdG, and Maxwell/mixed sectors;
  • on the isotropic branch the Stage-5 formulas reduce exactly to
    u2 = -D2/D0, u4 = (D2^2 - D0 D4)/D0^2, P0 = N0/D0,
    and the universal normalization test is
    mhat_0^2 P0 = 54 G c_s^5 / (5 a^5 c^5);
  • the constant-prefactor branch conditions are
    N2 = 2 D2 N0 / D0 and
    N4 = [2 D0(D2 N2 + D4 N0) - 3 D2^2 N0]/D0^2;
  • to first order around isotropy,
    delta u2 = -(delta D2 + u2 delta D0)/D0,
    delta P0 = (delta N0 - P0 delta D0)/D0,
    so conservative or transfer anisotropy can be tracked separately;
  • and on a stable isotropic branch, increasing N0 or increasing the conservative
    support softening B0,Z0 raises P0, while increasing K lowers it.

  • the mixed 4+1 Maxwell fields E_w = F_w0 and C_a = F_aw are gauge invariant;
  • a reduced wall + brane-like Maxwell mode + mixed A_w/F_{mu w}/J^w-active mode has the exact conservative self-energy
    Sigma_EM+mix^cons = [g_A^2 W + 2 g_A g_W R + g_W^2 A] / (A W - R^2);
  • its low-frequency coefficients are explicit rational functions of the conservative pole data and couplings;
  • dressing the mixed block by a retarded port Pi_out transfers the odd part to the wall with coefficient
    N(0) = (Omega_A^2 g_W + R g_A)^2 / (Omega_A^2 Omega_W^2 - R^2)^2 >= 0;
  • the compact outgoing l=2 branch has the normalized fingerprint
    Y2_hat = 1 + a^2 omega^2/(9 c_s^2) + 4 a^4 omega^4/(81 c_s^4) + i a^5 omega^5/(27 c_s^5) + ...;
  • therefore the first wall-level odd quadrupole coefficient is the outgoing-port coefficient
    a^5/(27 c_s^5) multiplied by the conservative mixed-sector transfer factor N(0);
  • if the scalar outlet enters only through a derivative mixed coupling, the wall-level scalar odd term starts at i*omega^3 rather than i*omega.

+```python
+    # Schur-complement derivation of the rational form used below.
+    # The (U,W) block of the Lagrangian (paper eq:app-stage023-full-lagrangian

+    #     M(ω) = [[Ω_U^2 - ω^2, R], [R, Ω_W^2 - ω^2]]

+    # which must equal the (Q - H ω^2)/det_M rational form used in Section II.0.
